# Tinkering — internal recipe candidates

**43 candidates; validation, public projection and exact review pending.** Original source fields remain unchanged.

## Field Caustic Trap — Black Dragon Fang — recipe-585

**Internal candidate; public; exact approval pending.**

Use the [complete operating guide](ADVANCED_CRAFT_INTERNAL_OPERATIONAL_GUIDE.md) and [body catalogue](ADVANCED_CRAFT_INTERNAL_BODY_CATALOGUE.md).

**Effect and operation**

This fixed trap has 1 charge(s). Spend one minute with both hands and actual Tinker's tools to place it on a stable eligible unoccupied 5-foot square, then an Action to arm; it becomes armed at the next regular-turn start or six-second boundary. Its first actual creature entrant, including allies, consumes one charge and the setter's available shared offensive allowance: DC 11 DEX, 1d8 acid on failure, zero on success. It then disarms. No discharge, charge spend or queued attack when the setter's allowance is unavailable. Forced movement/fall/teleport arrival can be entry; remaining in place is not. Moving it disarms it. Max three armed crafted traps per setter; manual reset, cleared square, delay and one-item-per-round guard remain. Setter incapacity/death alone does not switch off an eligible automatic trap. TI-02 gives full disarm rules. TI-06's trained one-hour servicing may refill charges; HP repair/rest may not.

**Complete output bodies**

Choose before any work: TI-trap: Steel trigger plate, captive spring/striker and calibrated discharge block; no loose expendable ammunition. 10 gp, 8 lb, ordinary B5, structural AC 19, dimensions 12 × 12 × 3 inches. See the complete body catalogue for original primaries, constituent masses, hands, capacity and support. A creature output replaces stock object HP/AC with its printed creature statistics. EN alternate weapon/suit hosts use their exact registered existing equipment profile; these default figures do not overwrite that alternate body.

**Before creation**

Reserve 3 actual complete never-magically-altered bodies, one model/size per order, in an exclusive fixed order. All must be complete and positive-HP immediately before the completion roll, even if fewer outputs will result. Bodies are additional to the exact source batch components and working tools. Pre-roll lawful replacement keeps its ordered position; no post-roll acquisition, substitution or reordering. Transform the first resolved outputs; unused bodies remain actual mundane stock. Record material identity, condition, real storage, actual station capacity, preservation and safe handling; a quoted price supplies none of those facts.

**Creation check and work**

Tinkering level 2; INT with actual Tinker's tools training, one PB or replacing expertise, DC 11. Untrained otherwise eligible level 1–4 uses no tool PB and disadvantage; trained recipe eligibility is max(4, trade level), with actual formula access. Source duration 2 hours; operational 120 active minutes, or 90 for an eligible Forge Dwarf. The included first stable phase is 15 minutes (15 for that Dwarf); remaining work is 105/75 minutes respectively, in declared phases of at most 480 minutes. Only actual incorporated inputs stabilize; raw/returned catalyst clocks continue. Interruption loses unfinished-phase progress, not costs or completed work.

**Facility and assistance**

Actual Tinker's Workshop, with Tinker's tools, Precision drivers, Calibration gauge; fieldCraftable=false, source timeMode=field. A field label never overrides the false creation flag. Fixed compatible facilities, actual minimum surface/bay/fixtures, safe access and expedition-site permissions remain required. Maximum 0 eligible assistants work all required hours without shortening the task or gaining assistant XP. Ordinary facilities/tools grant +0. Two active work Watches, no overlapping labor/rest credit, actual hired capacity and lawful access still apply.

**Formula and advancement**

Preserve the exact discovery and progression records: {"discovery": {"sources": ["trainer", "starter manual", "common experimentation"], "minimumResearchHours": 0, "requiresFormula": false, "formulaConsumed": false}, "skillProgression": {"skillXPOnSuccess": 4, "trivialAtSkillLevel": 7, "firstCraftBonus": true}}. The crafting framework determines actual first/repeat/qualified-failure/triviality XP. Source skillXPOnSuccess is not an extra award on use, repair, study, service or temporary class fabrication. A finished item supplies no formula/proficiency.

**Manufacturing outcomes**

Mishap 0; Failed 0; Flawed 1; Standard 2; Exceptional yield 2; Masterwork yield 3. Natural 1 is Mishap; natural 20 adds no band shift. An eligible Forge Dwarf's Standard becomes Exceptional. Alternatively retain 2 structural objects with the strongest P25/P50 workmanship once. Maximum = B + max(1, floor(B×P/100)) for positive P, else B; one existing/new singleton persistent flaw halves last, minimum 1. Preserve old maximum minus current damage deficit, clamping the new current to [0,newMax]. Q1 Flawed creates one persistent flaw; Q≥2 reduced yield adds no new singleton flaw, even when one output remains. Standard never heals. Actual EN host size/grade B takes precedence over default-body examples. No outcome improves charges, potency, source DC, spell rank, capacities or permissions; later repair does not remove a flaw or reapply manufacturing quality.

**Exact component occurrences**

All listed components are for the original whole batch, not per output. Repeated catalog IDs remain separate supplied quantities.

- [0] Resonance Cell (ing-585-0-resonance_cell): quantity 5; tier 1; source unit 2 gp / 0.1 lb; role ingredient; consumed=true.
- [1] black dragon fang (drop-black_dragon_fang): quantity 1; tier 1; source unit 5 gp / 0.67 lb; role material; consumed=true.

**Retention and failure**

This recipe has no eligible catalyst-return occurrence. Failed creation recovers 25% of genuinely remaining consumed non-catalyst quantities by exact identity and legal unit precision, capped by actual remains. The per-occurrence record identifies only expressly divisible bulk; otherwise floor to indivisible units. No quarter tool/lens/core, money conversion, duplicate catalyst, or second salvage pass over waste. Mishap/abandonment returns no consumed inputs. Retained working tools and unused complete bodies survive only as they actually exist. Returned material retains real age, vessels and history; a retained catalyst is not also inside the finished item.

**Restoration and reference prices**

TI-trap: finished unit 97/2 gp, functional repair material 5/2 gp and default 1 hours at DC 10; recoverable Broken/wreck reconstruction material 97/8 gp and default 8 hours at original DC 11. Recompute actual time from current recorded maximum HP: max(1, maxHP/10) or max(8, maxHP/10). Alternate admitted host price/size/grade uses its actual reference. Trained primary trade + INT, original station/implements and host-compatible repair fixtures are required. Original creation record and all magic-bearing primaries must survive; missing/burnt pages, missing frame/actuator, sacrificed or already-consumed debris are not reconstructible by payment. Core task natural shifts collapse to success/failure; failure spends time/material. Restore only recorded HP and lawful function, never charges/service/spell uses, flaws or prior deadlines. No Forge reduction, quality, extra generic assistants or XP. Reference = original batch market value / original Q + actual body; keep exact fractions until final copper purchase-ceiling/sale-floor. Actual quotes, availability, service and ownership are separate.

**Separate servicing**

TI-trap: actual maintenance pack 1 gp / 2/25 lb. End active operation first; with the complete positive-HP body, actual trained Tinker's tools, a genuinely safe surface and one active hour, make INT tool DC 10 as a Core binary task. Success sets the printed maximum to 1; failure spends the pack/work. No HP repair, surplus, XP, Forge discount or reset of prior round/block history. Field servicing can qualify here while field creation remains false. TI-06 governs pack identity, procedure and failed materials.

**State and interaction**

Initialize each actual output’s budget once at completion. Track physical identity, controller/holder, complete structure, current HP, suppression/meld, permissions, exact clock/expedition/round events and expenditures. Only actual legal commitment spends resources; legal committed misses/prevention/interruption do not refund them. No transfers, snapshots, copies, repair or relabeling reset state. The full 48-card Tinker interface register and TD-01–18 apply; class-only bombs, turrets, Anacite modules, deployment costs and caps are not recipe grants. All pending feat interactions remain unapproved. Use the complete shared guide alongside this card. Source titles/donors/private research do not create targets, facts, custody, permissions or cosmology.

**Unchanged source effect**

Deployable device with 1 charge; when triggered, a target makes a DC 11 Dexterity save or takes 1d8 acid damage.

## Superior Venom Clockwork of Spore Pod — recipe-586

**Internal candidate; public; exact approval pending.**

Use the [complete operating guide](ADVANCED_CRAFT_INTERNAL_OPERATIONAL_GUIDE.md) and [body catalogue](ADVANCED_CRAFT_INTERNAL_BODY_CATALOGUE.md).

**Effect and operation**

Choose Tiny or Small before work and keep that size. The finished Construct has AC 12 and maximum 16 HP; full unmodified B5 chassis gives full HP, otherwise initial HP is max(1, floor(16 × latest chassis HP / 5)). Its fixed magical nonspell melee attack is +3 to hit, reach 5 feet, 1d6 poison on hit, no ability damage or condition rider. Ordinary attack natural results apply. Tiny: STR 6, DEX 12, CON 10, INT 3, WIS 10, CHA 1, walk 20 feet, gripper load at most 5 lb. Small: STR 12, DEX 10, CON 12, INT 3, WIS 10, CHA 1, walk 25 feet, at most 20 lb. Ordinary senses/passive 10; no flight, PB, equipment slots or Anacite modules. Poison damage and Poisoned immunity only; other defenses are not inferred. Maximum service units 1: one genuine encounter for at most ten elapsed minutes, or one ten-minute noncombat operating block, paid at block start. Continuing past expiry needs another; entering combat closes the noncombat block and spends a new unit. Zero units inside an already paid block permits that block only. Suppression/shutdown keeps the clock running. TI-04 binds a capable consenting INT-6+ controller with ten minutes of real tools/permission, max three active ordinary crafted automata separately from one 686. One owner-wide Bonus Action command and one relevant offensive allowance per numbered round across class/crafted devices; actual communication within 60 feet, execution after the whole controller turn, no independent Reaction. The commanded creature moves and makes one attack or permitted task; powered uncommanded behavior is only defensive movement/Dodge. Transfer cannot give a second attack. Finite noncombat programming is ten minutes, with Action start and paid blocks; no professional crafting, script use or ram crew. At zero HP it is a destroyed recoverable wreck if original primaries survive; no death saves, Wounds or ordinary biological healing. TI-06 servicing and AC-09/TD-07 restoration are distinct. Structural quality never changes the printed creature statistics. TI-03–07 govern the complete procedure.

## Superior Mighty Trap of Splintered Bone — recipe-588

**Internal candidate; public; exact approval pending.**

Use the [complete operating guide](ADVANCED_CRAFT_INTERNAL_OPERATIONAL_GUIDE.md) and [body catalogue](ADVANCED_CRAFT_INTERNAL_BODY_CATALOGUE.md).

**Effect and operation**

This fixed trap has 1 charge(s). Spend one minute with both hands and actual Tinker's tools to place it on a stable eligible unoccupied 5-foot square, then an Action to arm; it becomes armed at the next regular-turn start or six-second boundary. Its first actual creature entrant, including allies, consumes one charge and the setter's available shared offensive allowance: DC 11 DEX, 1d8 force on failure, zero on success. It then disarms. No discharge, charge spend or queued attack when the setter's allowance is unavailable. Forced movement/fall/teleport arrival can be entry; remaining in place is not. Moving it disarms it. Max three armed crafted traps per setter; manual reset, cleared square, delay and one-item-per-round guard remain. Setter incapacity/death alone does not switch off an eligible automatic trap. TI-02 gives full disarm rules. TI-06's trained one-hour servicing may refill charges; HP repair/rest may not.

**Complete output bodies**

Choose before any work: TI-trap: Steel trigger plate, captive spring/striker and calibrated discharge block; no loose expendable ammunition. 10 gp, 8 lb, ordinary B5, structural AC 19, dimensions 12 × 12 × 3 inches. See the complete body catalogue for original primaries, constituent masses, hands, capacity and support. A creature output replaces stock object HP/AC with its printed creature statistics. EN alternate weapon/suit hosts use their exact registered existing equipment profile; these default figures do not overwrite that alternate body.

**Before creation**

Reserve 3 actual complete never-magically-altered bodies, one model/size per order, in an exclusive fixed order. All must be complete and positive-HP immediately before the completion roll, even if fewer outputs will result. Bodies are additional to the exact source batch components and working tools. Pre-roll lawful replacement keeps its ordered position; no post-roll acquisition, substitution or reordering. Transform the first resolved outputs; unused bodies remain actual mundane stock. Record material identity, condition, real storage, actual station capacity, preservation and safe handling; a quoted price supplies none of those facts.

**Creation check and work**

Tinkering level 5; INT with actual Tinker's tools training, one PB or replacing expertise, DC 11. Untrained otherwise eligible level 1–4 uses no tool PB and disadvantage; trained recipe eligibility is max(4, trade level), with actual formula access. Source duration 4 hours; operational 240 active minutes, or 180 for an eligible Forge Dwarf. The included first stable phase is 15 minutes (15 for that Dwarf); remaining work is 225/165 minutes respectively, in declared phases of at most 480 minutes. Only actual incorporated inputs stabilize; raw/returned catalyst clocks continue. Interruption loses unfinished-phase progress, not costs or completed work.

**Facility and assistance**

Actual Tinker's Workshop, with Tinker's tools, Precision drivers, Calibration gauge; fieldCraftable=false, source timeMode=expedition. A field label never overrides the false creation flag. Fixed compatible facilities, actual minimum surface/bay/fixtures, safe access and expedition-site permissions remain required. Maximum 0 eligible assistants work all required hours without shortening the task or gaining assistant XP. Ordinary facilities/tools grant +0. Two active work Watches, no overlapping labor/rest credit, actual hired capacity and lawful access still apply.

**Formula and advancement**

Preserve the exact discovery and progression records: {"discovery": {"sources": ["guild trainer", "faction vendor", "uncommon dungeon book", "reverse engineering"], "minimumResearchHours": 0, "requiresFormula": true, "formulaConsumed": false}, "skillProgression": {"skillXPOnSuccess": 4, "trivialAtSkillLevel": 10, "firstCraftBonus": true}}. The crafting framework determines actual first/repeat/qualified-failure/triviality XP. Source skillXPOnSuccess is not an extra award on use, repair, study, service or temporary class fabrication. A finished item supplies no formula/proficiency.

**Manufacturing outcomes**

Mishap 0; Failed 0; Flawed 1; Standard 2; Exceptional yield 2; Masterwork yield 3. Natural 1 is Mishap; natural 20 adds no band shift. An eligible Forge Dwarf's Standard becomes Exceptional. Alternatively retain 2 structural objects with the strongest P25/P50 workmanship once. Maximum = B + max(1, floor(B×P/100)) for positive P, else B; one existing/new singleton persistent flaw halves last, minimum 1. Preserve old maximum minus current damage deficit, clamping the new current to [0,newMax]. Q1 Flawed creates one persistent flaw; Q≥2 reduced yield adds no new singleton flaw, even when one output remains. Standard never heals. Actual EN host size/grade B takes precedence over default-body examples. No outcome improves charges, potency, source DC, spell rank, capacities or permissions; later repair does not remove a flaw or reapply manufacturing quality.

**Exact component occurrences**

All listed components are for the original whole batch, not per output. Repeated catalog IDs remain separate supplied quantities.

- [0] Screws (ing-588-0-screws): quantity 4; tier 3; source unit 25 gp / 0.1 lb; role binder; consumed=true.
- [1] Resonance Cell (ing-588-1-resonance_cell): quantity 4; tier 3; source unit 25 gp / 0.1 lb; role ingredient; consumed=true.
- [2] Metal frame (ing-588-2-metal_frame): quantity 3; tier 3; source unit 25 gp / 0.1 lb; role substrate; consumed=true.
- [3] splintered bone (drop-splintered_bone): quantity 1; tier 1; source unit 6 gp / 0.14 lb; role material; consumed=true.

**Retention and failure**

This recipe has no eligible catalyst-return occurrence. Failed creation recovers 25% of genuinely remaining consumed non-catalyst quantities by exact identity and legal unit precision, capped by actual remains. The per-occurrence record identifies only expressly divisible bulk; otherwise floor to indivisible units. No quarter tool/lens/core, money conversion, duplicate catalyst, or second salvage pass over waste. Mishap/abandonment returns no consumed inputs. Retained working tools and unused complete bodies survive only as they actually exist. Returned material retains real age, vessels and history; a retained catalyst is not also inside the finished item.

**Restoration and reference prices**

TI-trap: finished unit 409/2 gp, functional repair material 5/2 gp and default 1 hours at DC 10; recoverable Broken/wreck reconstruction material 409/8 gp and default 8 hours at original DC 11. Recompute actual time from current recorded maximum HP: max(1, maxHP/10) or max(8, maxHP/10). Alternate admitted host price/size/grade uses its actual reference. Trained primary trade + INT, original station/implements and host-compatible repair fixtures are required. Original creation record and all magic-bearing primaries must survive; missing/burnt pages, missing frame/actuator, sacrificed or already-consumed debris are not reconstructible by payment. Core task natural shifts collapse to success/failure; failure spends time/material. Restore only recorded HP and lawful function, never charges/service/spell uses, flaws or prior deadlines. No Forge reduction, quality, extra generic assistants or XP. Reference = original batch market value / original Q + actual body; keep exact fractions until final copper purchase-ceiling/sale-floor. Actual quotes, availability, service and ownership are separate.

**Separate servicing**

TI-trap: actual maintenance pack 1 gp / 2/25 lb. End active operation first; with the complete positive-HP body, actual trained Tinker's tools, a genuinely safe surface and one active hour, make INT tool DC 10 as a Core binary task. Success sets the printed maximum to 1; failure spends the pack/work. No HP repair, surplus, XP, Forge discount or reset of prior round/block history. Field servicing can qualify here while field creation remains false. TI-06 governs pack identity, procedure and failed materials.

**State and interaction**

Initialize each actual output’s budget once at completion. Track physical identity, controller/holder, complete structure, current HP, suppression/meld, permissions, exact clock/expedition/round events and expenditures. Only actual legal commitment spends resources; legal committed misses/prevention/interruption do not refund them. No transfers, snapshots, copies, repair or relabeling reset state. The full 48-card Tinker interface register and TD-01–18 apply; class-only bombs, turrets, Anacite modules, deployment costs and caps are not recipe grants. All pending feat interactions remain unapproved. Use the complete shared guide alongside this card. Source titles/donors/private research do not create targets, facts, custody, permissions or cosmology.

**Effect and operation**

Choose Tiny or Small before work and keep that size. The finished Construct has AC 12 and maximum 16 HP; full unmodified B5 chassis gives full HP, otherwise initial HP is max(1, floor(16 × latest chassis HP / 5)). Its fixed magical nonspell melee attack is +3 to hit, reach 5 feet, 1d6 force on hit, no ability damage or condition rider. Ordinary attack natural results apply. Tiny: STR 6, DEX 12, CON 10, INT 3, WIS 10, CHA 1, walk 20 feet, gripper load at most 5 lb. Small: STR 12, DEX 10, CON 12, INT 3, WIS 10, CHA 1, walk 25 feet, at most 20 lb. Ordinary senses/passive 10; no flight, PB, equipment slots or Anacite modules. Poison damage and Poisoned immunity only; other defenses are not inferred. Maximum service units 1: one genuine encounter for at most ten elapsed minutes, or one ten-minute noncombat operating block, paid at block start. Continuing past expiry needs another; entering combat closes the noncombat block and spends a new unit. Zero units inside an already paid block permits that block only. Suppression/shutdown keeps the clock running. TI-04 binds a capable consenting INT-6+ controller with ten minutes of real tools/permission, max three active ordinary crafted automata separately from one 686. One owner-wide Bonus Action command and one relevant offensive allowance per numbered round across class/crafted devices; actual communication within 60 feet, execution after the whole controller turn, no independent Reaction. The commanded creature moves and makes one attack or permitted task; powered uncommanded behavior is only defensive movement/Dodge. Transfer cannot give a second attack. Finite noncombat programming is ten minutes, with Action start and paid blocks; no professional crafting, script use or ram crew. At zero HP it is a destroyed recoverable wreck if original primaries survive; no death saves, Wounds or ordinary biological healing. TI-06 servicing and AC-09/TD-07 restoration are distinct. Structural quality never changes the printed creature statistics. TI-03–07 govern the complete procedure.

Max three armed crafted traps per setter; manual reset, cleared square, delay and one-item-per-round guard remain. Setter incapacity/death alone does not switch off an eligible automatic trap. Choose Tiny or Small before work and keep that size. The finished Construct has AC 12 and maximum 14 HP; full unmodified B5 chassis gives full HP, otherwise initial HP is max(1, floor(14 × latest chassis HP / 5)). Its fixed magical nonspell melee attack is +3 to hit, reach 5 feet, 1d6 necrotic on hit, no ability damage or condition rider. Ordinary attack natural results apply. Tiny: STR 6, DEX 12, CON 10, INT 3, WIS 10, CHA 1, walk 20 feet, gripper load at most 5 lb. Small: STR 12, DEX 10, CON 12, INT 3, WIS 10, CHA 1, walk 25 feet, at most 20 lb. Ordinary senses/passive 10; no flight, PB, equipment slots or Anacite modules. Poison damage and Poisoned immunity only; other defenses are not inferred. Maximum service units 1: one genuine encounter for at most ten elapsed minutes, or one ten-minute noncombat operating block, paid at block start. Continuing past expiry needs another; entering combat closes the noncombat block and spends a new unit. Zero units inside an already paid block permits that block only. Suppression/shutdown keeps the clock running. TI-04 binds a capable consenting INT-6+ controller with ten minutes of real tools/permission, max three active ordinary crafted automata separately from one 686. One owner-wide Bonus Action command and one relevant offensive allowance per numbered round across class/crafted devices; actual communication within 60 feet, execution after the whole controller turn, no independent Reaction. The commanded creature moves and makes one attack or permitted task; powered uncommanded behavior is only defensive movement/Dodge. Transfer cannot give a second attack. Finite noncombat programming is ten minutes, with Action start and paid blocks; no professional crafting, script use or ram crew. At zero HP it is a destroyed recoverable wreck if original primaries survive; no death saves, Wounds or ordinary biological healing. TI-06 servicing and AC-09/TD-07 restoration are distinct. Structural quality never changes the printed creature statistics. TI-03–07 govern the complete procedure.

## Refined Storm Clockwork — recipe-594

**Effect and operation**

Choose Tiny or Small before work and keep that size. The finished Construct has AC 12 and maximum 14 HP; full unmodified B5 chassis gives full HP, otherwise initial HP is max(1, floor(14 × latest chassis HP / 5)). Its fixed magical nonspell melee attack is +3 to hit, reach 5 feet, 1d6 lightning on hit, no ability damage or condition rider. Ordinary attack natural results apply. Tiny: STR 6, DEX 12, CON 10, INT 3, WIS 10, CHA 1, walk 20 feet, gripper load at most 5 lb. Small: STR 12, DEX 10, CON 12, INT 3, WIS 10, CHA 1, walk 25 feet, at most 20 lb. Ordinary senses/passive 10; no flight, PB, equipment slots or Anacite modules. Poison damage and Poisoned immunity only; other defenses are not inferred. Maximum service units 1: one genuine encounter for at most ten elapsed minutes, or one ten-minute noncombat operating block, paid at block start. Continuing past expiry needs another; entering combat closes the noncombat block and spends a new unit. Zero units inside an already paid block permits that block only. Suppression/shutdown keeps the clock running. TI-04 binds a capable consenting INT-6+ controller with ten minutes of real tools/permission, max three active ordinary crafted automata separately from one 686. One owner-wide Bonus Action command and one relevant offensive allowance per numbered round across class/crafted devices; actual communication within 60 feet, execution after the whole controller turn, no independent Reaction. The commanded creature moves and makes one attack or permitted task; powered uncommanded behavior is only defensive movement/Dodge. Transfer cannot give a second attack. Finite noncombat programming is ten minutes, with Action start and paid blocks; no professional crafting, script use or ram crew. At zero HP it is a destroyed recoverable wreck if original primaries survive; no death saves, Wounds or ordinary biological healing. TI-06 servicing and AC-09/TD-07 restoration are distinct. Structural quality never changes the printed creature statistics. TI-03–07 govern the complete procedure.

**Complete output bodies**

Choose before any work: TI-auto-tiny: Mundane pre-animation Tiny chassis; finished creature uses source AC/HP, not this stock HP/AC. 25 gp, 8 lb, ordinary B5, structural AC 19, dimensions 12 × 8 × 8 inches; TI-auto-small: Mundane pre-animation Small chassis; finished creature uses source AC/HP, not this stock HP/AC. 50 gp, 25 lb, ordinary B5, structural AC 19, dimensions 24 × 16 × 20 inches. This fixed trap has 1 charge(s). Spend one minute with both hands and actual Tinker's tools to place it on a stable eligible unoccupied 5-foot square, then an Action to arm; it becomes armed at the next regular-turn start or six-second boundary. Its first actual creature entrant, including allies, consumes one charge and the setter's available shared offensive allowance: DC 8 DEX, 1d8 cold on failure, zero on success. It then disarms. No discharge, charge spend or queued attack when the setter's allowance is unavailable. Forced movement/fall/teleport arrival can be entry; remaining in place is not. Moving it disarms it. Max three armed crafted traps per setter; manual reset, cleared square, delay and one-item-per-round guard remain. Setter incapacity/death alone does not switch off an eligible automatic trap. ## Superior Caustic Automaton of Acidic Gland — recipe-600

**Internal candidate; public; exact approval pending.**

**Effect and operation**

Choose Tiny or Small before work and keep that size. The finished Construct has AC 13 and maximum 26 HP; full unmodified B5 chassis gives full HP, otherwise initial HP is max(1, floor(26 × latest chassis HP / 5)). Its fixed magical nonspell melee attack is +4 to hit, reach 5 feet, 2d6 acid on hit, no ability damage or condition rider. Ordinary attack natural results apply. Tiny: STR 6, DEX 12, CON 10, INT 3, WIS 10, CHA 1, walk 20 feet, gripper load at most 5 lb. Small: STR 12, DEX 10, CON 12, INT 3, WIS 10, CHA 1, walk 25 feet, at most 20 lb. Ordinary senses/passive 10; no flight, PB, equipment slots or Anacite modules. Poison damage and Poisoned immunity only; other defenses are not inferred. Maximum service units 2: one genuine encounter for at most ten elapsed minutes, or one ten-minute noncombat operating block, paid at block start. Continuing past expiry needs another; entering combat closes the noncombat block and spends a new unit. Zero units inside an already paid block permits that block only. Suppression/shutdown keeps the clock running. TI-04 binds a capable consenting INT-6+ controller with ten minutes of real tools/permission, max three active ordinary crafted automata separately from one 686. One owner-wide Bonus Action command and one relevant offensive allowance per numbered round across class/crafted devices; actual communication within 60 feet, execution after the whole controller turn, no independent Reaction. The commanded creature moves and makes one attack or permitted task; powered uncommanded behavior is only defensive movement/Dodge. Transfer cannot give a second attack. Finite noncombat programming is ten minutes, with Action start and paid blocks; no professional crafting, script use or ram crew. At zero HP it is a destroyed recoverable wreck if original primaries survive; no death saves, Wounds or ordinary biological healing. TI-06 servicing and AC-09/TD-07 restoration are distinct. Structural quality never changes the printed creature statistics. TI-03–07 govern the complete procedure.

Its first actual creature entrant, including allies, consumes one charge and the setter's available shared offensive allowance: DC 17 DEX, 2d8 force on failure, zero on success. It then disarms. No discharge, charge spend or queued attack when the setter's allowance is unavailable. Forced movement/fall/teleport arrival can be entry; remaining in place is not. Moving it disarms it. **Effect and operation**

Choose Tiny or Small before work and keep that size. The finished Construct has AC 13 and maximum 28 HP; full unmodified B5 chassis gives full HP, otherwise initial HP is max(1, floor(28 × latest chassis HP / 5)). Its fixed magical nonspell melee attack is +4 to hit, reach 5 feet, 2d6 bludgeoning on hit, no ability damage or condition rider. Ordinary attack natural results apply. Tiny: STR 6, DEX 12, CON 10, INT 3, WIS 10, CHA 1, walk 20 feet, gripper load at most 5 lb. Small: STR 12, DEX 10, CON 12, INT 3, WIS 10, CHA 1, walk 25 feet, at most 20 lb. Ordinary senses/passive 10; no flight, PB, equipment slots or Anacite modules. Poison damage and Poisoned immunity only; other defenses are not inferred. Maximum service units 2: one genuine encounter for at most ten elapsed minutes, or one ten-minute noncombat operating block, paid at block start. Continuing past expiry needs another; entering combat closes the noncombat block and spends a new unit. Zero units inside an already paid block permits that block only. Suppression/shutdown keeps the clock running. TI-04 binds a capable consenting INT-6+ controller with ten minutes of real tools/permission, max three active ordinary crafted automata separately from one 686. One owner-wide Bonus Action command and one relevant offensive allowance per numbered round across class/crafted devices; actual communication within 60 feet, execution after the whole controller turn, no independent Reaction. The commanded creature moves and makes one attack or permitted task; powered uncommanded behavior is only defensive movement/Dodge. Transfer cannot give a second attack. Finite noncombat programming is ten minutes, with Action start and paid blocks; no professional crafting, script use or ram crew. At zero HP it is a destroyed recoverable wreck if original primaries survive; no death saves, Wounds or ordinary biological healing. TI-06 servicing and AC-09/TD-07 restoration are distinct. Structural quality never changes the printed creature statistics. TI-03–07 govern the complete procedure.

**Effect and operation**

This fixed trap has 3 charge(s). Spend one minute with both hands and actual Tinker's tools to place it on a stable eligible unoccupied 5-foot square, then an Action to arm; it becomes armed at the next regular-turn start or six-second boundary. Its first actual creature entrant, including allies, consumes one charge and the setter's available shared offensive allowance: DC 20 DEX, 4d8 cold on failure, zero on success. It then disarms. No discharge, charge spend or queued attack when the setter's allowance is unavailable. Forced movement/fall/teleport arrival can be entry; remaining in place is not. Moving it disarms it. Max three armed crafted traps per setter; manual reset, cleared square, delay and one-item-per-round guard remain. Setter incapacity/death alone does not switch off an eligible automatic trap. TI-02 gives full disarm rules. TI-06's trained one-hour servicing may refill charges; HP repair/rest may not.

Choose Tiny or Small before work and keep that size. The finished Construct has AC 15 and maximum 56 HP; full unmodified B5 chassis gives full HP, otherwise initial HP is max(1, floor(56 × latest chassis HP / 5)). Its fixed magical nonspell melee attack is +6 to hit, reach 5 feet, 4d6 poison on hit, no ability damage or condition rider. Ordinary attack natural results apply. Tiny: STR 6, DEX 12, CON 10, INT 3, WIS 10, CHA 1, walk 20 feet, gripper load at most 5 lb. Small: STR 12, DEX 10, CON 12, INT 3, WIS 10, CHA 1, walk 25 feet, at most 20 lb. Ordinary senses/passive 10; no flight, PB, equipment slots or Anacite modules. Poison damage and Poisoned immunity only; other defenses are not inferred. Maximum service units 4: one genuine encounter for at most ten elapsed minutes, or one ten-minute noncombat operating block, paid at block start. Continuing past expiry needs another; entering combat closes the noncombat block and spends a new unit. Zero units inside an already paid block permits that block only. Suppression/shutdown keeps the clock running. TI-04 binds a capable consenting INT-6+ controller with ten minutes of real tools/permission, max three active ordinary crafted automata separately from one 686. One owner-wide Bonus Action command and one relevant offensive allowance per numbered round across class/crafted devices; actual communication within 60 feet, execution after the whole controller turn, no independent Reaction. The commanded creature moves and makes one attack or permitted task; powered uncommanded behavior is only defensive movement/Dodge. Transfer cannot give a second attack. Finite noncombat programming is ten minutes, with Action start and paid blocks; no professional crafting, script use or ram crew. At zero HP it is a destroyed recoverable wreck if original primaries survive; no death saves, Wounds or ordinary biological healing. TI-06 servicing and AC-09/TD-07 restoration are distinct. Structural quality never changes the printed creature statistics. TI-03–07 govern the complete procedure.

## Transcendent Stone Clockwork of White Dragonheart — recipe-620

**Internal candidate; director-only; exact approval pending.**

Use the [complete operating guide](ADVANCED_CRAFT_INTERNAL_OPERATIONAL_GUIDE.md) and [body catalogue](ADVANCED_CRAFT_INTERNAL_BODY_CATALOGUE.md).

**Effect and operation**

Choose Tiny or Small before work and keep that size. The finished Construct has AC 16 and maximum 70 HP; full unmodified B5 chassis gives full HP, otherwise initial HP is max(1, floor(70 × latest chassis HP / 5)). Its fixed magical nonspell melee attack is +7 to hit, reach 5 feet, 5d6 bludgeoning on hit, no ability damage or condition rider. Ordinary attack natural results apply. Tiny: STR 6, DEX 12, CON 10, INT 3, WIS 10, CHA 1, walk 20 feet, gripper load at most 5 lb. Small: STR 12, DEX 10, CON 12, INT 3, WIS 10, CHA 1, walk 25 feet, at most 20 lb. Ordinary senses/passive 10; no flight, PB, equipment slots or Anacite modules. Poison damage and Poisoned immunity only; other defenses are not inferred. Maximum service units 5: one genuine encounter for at most ten elapsed minutes, or one ten-minute noncombat operating block, paid at block start. Continuing past expiry needs another; entering combat closes the noncombat block and spends a new unit. Zero units inside an already paid block permits that block only. Suppression/shutdown keeps the clock running. TI-04 binds a capable consenting INT-6+ controller with ten minutes of real tools/permission, max three active ordinary crafted automata separately from one 686. One owner-wide Bonus Action command and one relevant offensive allowance per numbered round across class/crafted devices; actual communication within 60 feet, execution after the whole controller turn, no independent Reaction. The commanded creature moves and makes one attack or permitted task; powered uncommanded behavior is only defensive movement/Dodge. Transfer cannot give a second attack. Finite noncombat programming is ten minutes, with Action start and paid blocks; no professional crafting, script use or ram crew. At zero HP it is a destroyed recoverable wreck if original primaries survive; no death saves, Wounds or ordinary biological healing. TI-06 servicing and AC-09/TD-07 restoration are distinct. Structural quality never changes the printed creature statistics. TI-03–07 govern the complete procedure.

**Complete output bodies**

Choose before any work: TI-auto-tiny: Mundane pre-animation Tiny chassis; finished creature uses source AC/HP, not this stock HP/AC. 25 gp, 8 lb, ordinary B5, structural AC 19, dimensions 12 × 8 × 8 inches; TI-auto-small: Mundane pre-animation Small chassis; finished creature uses source AC/HP, not this stock HP/AC. 50 gp, 25 lb, ordinary B5, structural AC 19, dimensions 24 × 16 × 20 inches. ## Soul-Circuit Command Drone — recipe-686

**Internal candidate; director-only; exact approval pending.**

Use the [complete operating guide](ADVANCED_CRAFT_INTERNAL_OPERATIONAL_GUIDE.md) and [body catalogue](ADVANCED_CRAFT_INTERNAL_BODY_CATALOGUE.md).

**Effect and operation**

This is the custom AC 18 / 80 HP Small command relay with no attack. Its winding hour, three authorized links and current shared command limits use TI-08 below.

**Complete custom procedure — TI-08**

KEEP **Small,AC18,HP80,three targets,120ft and the literal Silent Ones/Heartcell prohibition**. NEW body TI-drone has walk25ft, STR12,DEX10,CON12,INT6,WIS10,CHA1, ordinary sight/hearing, passivePerception10, no flight/telepathy/attack or Anacite stats. Its relative intelligence is a limited protocol processor, not an assertion about a soul or sentience. It can convey observed ordinary status, not hidden state or remote inventories.

**NEW power:** one minute of actual manual winding with both hands powers at most one elapsed hour; capped at one hour remaining. Rewinding requires stopping the drone and actual access, never banks hours. Suppression/pause does not stop that elapsed deadline. This is its own winding clock, not a source charges field, dawn recharge or restoration. A complete positive-HP chassis is required. Power alone authorizes no command or offense.

Register its capable controller under TI-04. During ten active minutes, link at most three actual consenting/authorized existing Constructs or dungeon mechanisms, each with an actual protocol, current controller/owner permission, physically compatible interface and lawful action profile. No hostile control check is supplied; unknown/hostile/forbidden links fail admission rather than being overcome by a high roll. Replace a link only after explicit release and the same real setup. A fourth cannot act by briefly joining an oversized list. Silent Ones and Heartcell systems are never eligible under this item, even with a purported permission flag; preserve those literal identifiers without deciding their taxonomy.

The controller may use their one normal Bonus Action command to send a directly coordinated instruction through686 to a linked target **within120ft of the drone with line of effect**. Controller-to-drone communication must itself satisfy TI-04's60ft actual channel. One hop only. Up to three linked targets may receive status/task selection information, but **only one commanded action** executes through that owner's shared allowance; the others gain no action, attack or offensive movement. Another independently controlling creature must pay its own actual commands/actions for its own target. Class drones retain their class-specific command range/eligibility and no new class module/cap from the relay.686's range supplies no exception to a target mechanism's own restricted control source.

Loss of link/range/power before execution fails that command without refund; restored contact grants only later ordinary commands. No shared save/DC improvement, RP transfer, battlefield turn, automatic turret shot, forced consent or permission to operate a mechanism whose actual procedure remains unknown is supplied.

## Eternal-Actuator Gate Ram — recipe-687

**Internal candidate; director-only; exact approval pending.**

Use the [complete operating guide](ADVANCED_CRAFT_INTERNAL_OPERATIONAL_GUIDE.md) and [body catalogue](ADVANCED_CRAFT_INTERNAL_BODY_CATALOGUE.md).

**Effect and operation**

This is the custom stationary ram: +6 melee attack within 5 feet for 12d10 force against actual doors, gates, barriers or Constructs. Its three-Action cycle and separate once-per-long-rest creator-statistic breach use TI-09 below.

**Complete custom procedure — TI-09**

KEEP portable siege body, **12d10 force** against the printed doors/gates/barriers/Constructs, and one magical-breach attempt per long rest using **the crafter's Engineering bonus**. NEW TI-ram body:80lb,100gp mundane reference,48×18×18in,steelAC19/structuralHP20, captive head/frame/screw. It has no movement mode, passenger space, free ammunition, independent turn, class turret identity or autonomous action. Actual strength/carrying/support and a clear5ft working approach are required. Mounting/unmounting takes five active minutes each with two hands on a sound surface; no free placement inside a closed passage.

**NEW normal attack:** one capable operator, both hands. Spend a **wind Action**, then an **align Action**, then a **discharge Action on three distinct successive-or-later own regular turns**. Alignment names one actually perceived legal target within5ft and line of effect; preserve that target/position until discharge. No helper/extra turn/Extra Attack/Gadget Sync shortens the cycle. An actual necessary delay retains a safe wound stage, but moving, changing operator or changing target cancels alignment and requires restarting the cycle; no stored unlimited attacks. Outside initiative the minimum three-action cycle is18 elapsed seconds of work. The actual physical mechanism is hand-powered; its source magic supplies the printed force event, not external power generation.

At discharge make a **NEW fixed+6 melee siege-item attack** against that target's actual creature/material/worn-target AC. Hit12d10force, no flat ability damage, natural1 miss/natural20 doubles attack dice. It cannot attack ordinary non-Construct creatures merely by calling them barriers. Objects retain actual compatibility/immunity/unbreakable source rules. It supplies no automatic destruction, Wound exception, area damage, forward movement or follow-through target. It is a direct operator Action, not a companion's commanded attack. The next strike needs a new complete cycle.

**NEW magical breach mode replaces that discharge, not an additional12d10 attack.** At the legal attempt, debit this physical ram's one long-rest entitlement. The acting user need not be the crafter but must follow the cycle and have a genuinely eligible magically sealed passage. Snapshot at original completion the crafter's **unbuffed INT plus actual Engineering PB/replacing expertise and any independently reviewed permanent non-item Engineering modifier**; no creation specialty, assistant, facility, temporary buff, utility item, borrowed class level or later buyer statistic. No Engineering proficiency means no Engineering PB. Preserve the actual contributing source records; a price or new creator ID does not fabricate the statistic.

Use that fixed modifier on one Core Engineering task collapsed to binary. The passage's actual eligible breach DC takes precedence; otherwise **NEW defaultDC28** for an ordinary nonunique magical passage seal that has no contrary immunity or special key-only rule. Unknown/unique/explicitly unbreachable systems require their actual profile, not an automatic favorable default. Failure spends the attempt with no opening. Success opens one at-most5ft passage in that local magical obstruction for **one elapsed minute**; it does not destroy a physical door, disable the entire installation or reveal protected lore. A physical door remains subject to actual ordinary breach/damage. The returning magical obstruction blocks new crossings at expiry and grants no cutting/crushing damage or automatic displacement to a creature already in the act of crossing. Actual more-specific seal behavior wins where independently provided. This is a new bounded local breach, not general Dispel Magic or permission concerning Silent Ones/Heartcell systems.

**NEW rest binding:** record one registered operator and this physical ram's spent entitlement. A refill requires that registered operator to complete a genuine beneficial eight-hour long rest, wholly after the last expenditure, with the ram present/accessible throughout. A legitimate change of operator takes ten active minutes with lawful custody and preserves the spent state; the new operator must then complete their own qualifying post-expenditure rest with this ram. Prior rest, an already rested buyer, the maker resting elsewhere, an extra resting assistant, repair or a second name supplies no refill. Only one ram refill event is recorded for that qualifying rest; simultaneous people cannot bank several. This permits ordinary transfer, including after an operator dies, without an immediate fresh breach. It adds no RP pool or effect from an unbeneficial rest. Ordinary wind/align/discharge and HP repair have no long-rest refresh dependency.

**Complete output bodies**

Choose before any work: TI-ram: Portable hand-wound steel siege frame with screw, compression bed and captive striking head; not a vehicle or autonomous attacker. 100 gp, 80 lb, ordinary B20, structural AC 19, dimensions 48 × 18 × 18 inches. See the complete body catalogue for original primaries, constituent masses, hands, capacity and support. A creature output replaces stock object HP/AC with its printed creature statistics.
